HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_196af60de3125b65d9a1","text":"3. **The denied equality is tested by the two gathering places that follow.** `س و ي:B001` anchors the fixed negation; in 32:19 and 32:20, both groups receive a *maʾwā* from `ء و ي:B001`, a place toward which one is gathered or sheltered. The packet supplies the repeated shelter word and opposite destinations. **Inferred arrow:** different present relations to trust and boundary -> different terminal gathering points. The next two ayat spatialize the inequality asserted here rather than leaving it abstract.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_20b04d15aec1a7419e2e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:18:8\",\"root_000766\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonraki iki ayette iki gruba aynı barınak sözcüğüyle zıt yerler verilmesi, burada reddedilen eşitliği soyut bir değer farkından somut varış noktaları ayrılığına dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_281c037e5115393929dc","text":"2. **The negation rejects outcome-level interchangeability, not common creaturehood.** `س و ي:B001` is equality, equivalence, and balance. The repeated *kāna* and matched indefinite predicates make the comparison maximally symmetrical, then *lā yastawūn* breaks only the proposed equivalence. **Inferred arrow:** comparable grammatical form does not entail equal operative state. The verse sharpens judgment by first granting a fair comparison frame and then identifying the decisive difference.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000766/B001","root_001156/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_64a2ff46e9911c304225","text":"1. **Security versus breakout from a containing order.** **ء م ن B001-B002** supplies secure trust and confirming assent. The packet's sole accepted **ف س ق B002** supplies the concrete image of a date emerging from its peel. **س و ي B001** then denies equivalence. I infer that the peel image assigns the فاسق the role of one who has broken out from a containing norm, while the believer remains in trusting relation. The contrast reads spatially as held alignment versus boundary-exit, without reducing either moral category to the image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_657d4942a778ede5af60","text":"Later 32:19-20 supplied the promised spatial proof: both groups receive a *maʾwā* from `ء و ي:B001`, but one gathers into garden-hospitality and the other into burning enclosure. That exact lexical symmetry made 32:18's “not equal” far more concrete than a general evaluative contrast.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B001","root_001332/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c4371bb5444453536021","text":"2. **Different states cannot be flattened into equal rank.** **ك و ن B001-B002** supplies being in a state and position or rank; its repetition creates formally parallel clauses, while **س و ي B001** rejects their equality. I infer that grammatical symmetry is used to expose substantive asymmetry. The ayah therefore warns that identical human form or shared present location does not make the two enacted states equivalent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_001156/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d260ecda365141274e52","text":"1. **The two designations encode opposed relations to a containing order.** `ء م ن:B001` supplies settled security and trust, reinforced by trusting assent in `ء م ن:B002`; the packet's sole branch for `ف س ق:B002` is a fresh date emerging from its peel. The balanced *man kāna ... ka-man kāna* puts the figures in formal parallel before denying equivalence. **Inferred arrow:** trusting settlement within the sign-governed relation versus emergence from its containing boundary -> divergent moral trajectories. *Fāsiq* becomes an image of breached containment, not merely a label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dbff23df538215cd7dda","text":"The next two ayat assign distinct shelters, and 32:20 repeatedly blocks attempted خروج from fire.
